<div><p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-align: center"><span>LAWS OF DELAWARE</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-align: center"><span>VOLUME 85</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-align: center"><span>CHAPTER 5</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-align: center"><span>153rd GENERAL ASSEMBLY</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-align: center"><span>FORMERLY</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-align: center"><span>HOUSE BILL NO. 10</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span> </span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-align: justify"><span>AN ACT CONCURRING IN PROPOSED AMENDMENTS TO THE DELAWARE CONSTITUTION RELATING TO TECHNICAL CORRECTIONS.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span> </span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span><span>WHEREAS, an amendment to the Delaware Constitution was proposed in the 152</span><span style="font-size: 6.67pt; vertical-align: super">nd</span><span> General Assembly, being Chapter 281 of Volume 84 of the Laws of Delaware (“proposed amendment”); and</span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>WHEREAS, the proposed amendment was adopted by two-thirds of all members elected to each house of the 152</span><span style="font-size: 6.67pt; vertical-align: super">nd</span><span> General Assembly; and</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>WHEREAS, following adoption by the General Assembly, the proposed amendment was publicized in accordance with the Delaware Constitution; and</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>WHEREAS, when the 153</span><span style="font-size: 6.67pt; vertical-align: super">rd</span><span> General Assembly concurs in the proposed amendment, the amendment will become part of the Delaware Constitution.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>NOW, THEREFORE:</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>BE IT ENACTED BY THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY OF THE STATE OF DELAWARE (Two-thirds of all members elected to each house thereof concurring therein):</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 1. Amend § 1, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. Freedom of religion.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 1.</span><span> Although it is the duty of all persons frequently to assemble together for the public worship of Almighty God; and piety and morality, on which the prosperity of communities depends, are hereby promoted; yet no person shall or ought to be compelled to attend any religious worship, to contribute to the erection or support of any place of worship, or to the maintenance of any ministry, against</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the person’s</span><span> own free will and consent; and no power shall or ought to be vested in or assumed by any magistrate that shall in any case interfere with, or in any manner control the rights of conscience, in the free exercise of religious worship, nor a preference given by law to any religious societies, denominations, or modes of worship</span><span></span><span></span><span>.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 2. Amend § 2, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2. Religious test for office not required.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2.</span><span> No religious test shall be required as a qualification to any office, or public trust, under this State.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 3. Amend § 3, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 3. Free and equal elections.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 3.</span><span> All elections shall be free and equal.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 4. Amend § 4, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 4. Trial by jury.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 4.</span><span> Trial by jury shall be as heretofore.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 5. Amend § 5, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 5. Freedom of press and speech; evidence in libel prosecutions; jury questions.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 5.</span><span> The free communication of thoughts and opinions is one of the invaluable rights of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> man.</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> humanity.</span><span> The press shall be free to every citizen who undertakes to examine the official conduct of persons acting in a public capacity; and any citizen may freely speak,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> write</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> write,</span><span> and print on any subject, being responsible for the abuse of that liberty. In prosecutions for publications, investigating the proceedings of officers, or where the matter published is proper for public information, the truth thereof may be given in evidence; and in all indictments for libels the jury may determine the facts and the law, as in other cases.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 6. Amend § 6, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 6. Searches and seizures.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 6.</span><span> The people shall be secure in their persons, houses,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> papers</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> papers,</span><span> and possessions, from unreasonable searches and seizures; and no warrant to search any place, or to seize any person or thing, shall issue without describing them as particularly as may be; nor then, unless there be probable cause supported by oath or affirmation.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 7. Amend § 7, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 7. Procedural rights in criminal prosecutions; jury trial; self-incrimination; deprivation of life,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> liberty</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> liberty,</span><span> or property.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 7.</span><span> In all criminal prosecutions, the accused </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">hath</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">has</span><span> a right to be heard</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> by himself or herself and his or her</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> personally and by</span><span> counsel, to be plainly and fully informed of the nature and cause of the accusation against</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> him or her,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the accused,</span><span> to meet the witnesses in their examination face to face, to have compulsory process in due time, on application by</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> himself or herself, his or her</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the accused, the accused’s</span><span> friends or counsel, for obtaining witnesses in</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the accused’s</span><span> favor, and a speedy and public trial by an impartial jury;</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> he or she</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the accused</span><span> shall not be compelled to give evidence against</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> himself or herself,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the accused’s own self,</span><span> nor shall</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> he or she</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the accused</span><span> be deprived of life,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> liberty</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> liberty,</span><span> or property, unless by the judgment of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the accused’s</span><span> peers or by the law of the land.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 8. Amend § 8, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 8. Prosecution by indictment or information; double jeopardy; just compensation for property.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 8.</span><span> No person shall for any indictable offense be proceeded against criminally by information, except in cases arising in the land or naval forces, or in the militia when in actual service in time of war or public danger; and no person shall be for the same offense twice put in jeopardy of life or limb; nor shall any person's property be taken or applied to public use without the consent of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the person’s</span><span> representatives, and without compensation being made. </span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 9. Amend § 9, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 9. Courts shall be open; remedy for injury; suits against State.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 9.</span><span> All courts shall be open; and every </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">person for an injury done him or her in his or her</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">individual for an injury done to the individual’s</span><span> reputation, person, </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">or</span><span> movable or immovable possessions, shall have remedy by the due course of law, and justice administered according to the very right of the cause and the law of the land, without sale, denial, or unreasonable delay or expense. Suits may be brought against</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State, according to such regulations as shall be made by law.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 10. Amend § 10, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 10. Suspension of laws by General Assembly.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 10.</span><span> No power of suspending laws shall be exercised but by authority of the General Assembly.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 11. Amend § 11, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 11. Excessive bail or fines; cruel punishments; health of prisoners.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 11.</span><span> Excessive bail shall not be required, nor excessive fines imposed, nor cruel punishments inflicted; and in the construction of jails a proper regard shall be had to the health of prisoners.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 12. Amend § 12, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 12. Right to bail; access to accused.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 12.</span><span> All prisoners shall be bailable by sufficient sureties, unless for capital offenses when the proof is positive or the presumption great; and when persons are confined on accusation for such offenses their friends and counsel may at proper seasons have access to them.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 13. Amend § 13, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 13. Suspension of habeas corpus.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 13. </span><span>The privilege of the writ of habeas corpus shall not be suspended, unless when in cases of rebellion or invasion the public safety may require it.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 14. Amend § 14, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 14. Commission of oyer and terminer, or jail delivery.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 14.</span><span> No commission of oyer and terminer, or jail delivery, shall be issued.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 15. Amend § 15, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 15. Corruption of blood; forfeiture; descent of suicide's estate.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 15.</span><span> No attainder shall work corruption of blood, nor except during the life of the offender forfeiture of estate. The estates of those who destroy their own lives shall descend or vest as in case of natural death, and if any person be killed by accident no forfeiture shall thereby be incurred.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 16. Amend § 16, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 16. Right of assembly; petition for redress of grievances.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 16.</span><span> Although disobedience to laws by a part of the people, upon suggestions of impolicy or injustice in them, tends by immediate effect and the influence of example not only to endanger the public welfare and safety, but also in governments of a republican form contravenes the social principles of such governments, founded on common consent for common good; yet the citizens have a right in an orderly manner to meet together, and to apply to persons</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> intrusted</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> entrusted</span><span> with the powers of government, for redress of grievances or other proper purposes, by petition,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> remonstrance</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> remonstrance,</span><span> or address.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 17. Amend § 17, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 17. Standing army; necessity for legislative consent; subordination of military.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 17.</span><span> No standing army shall be kept without the consent of the General Assembly, and the military</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> shall</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> shall,</span><span> in all cases and at all</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> times</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> times,</span><span> be in strict subordination to the civil power.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 18. Amend § 18, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 18. Prohibition against quartering soldiers in homes.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 18.</span><span> No soldier shall in time of peace be quartered in any house without the consent of the owner; nor in time of war but by a civil magistrate, in manner to be prescribed by law.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 19. Amend § 19, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 19. Hereditary distinctions; holding office during good behavior; offices and titles from foreign states.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 19.</span><span> No hereditary distinction shall be granted, nor any office created or exercised, the appointment to which shall be for a longer term than during good</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> behaviour;</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> behavior;</span><span> and no person holding any office under this State shall accept of any office or title of any kind whatever from any king, prince, or foreign State.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 20. Amend § 20, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 20. Right to keep and bear arms.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 20.</span><span> A person has the right to keep and bear arms for the defense of self, family, home and State, and for hunting and recreational use.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 21. Amend § 21, Article I of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 21. Equal Rights.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 21.</span><span> Equality of rights under the law shall not be denied or abridged on account of race, color, national origin, or sex.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 22. Amend § 1, Article II of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. General Assembly to hold legislative power; composition.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 1.</span><span> The legislative power of this State shall be vested in a General Assembly, which shall consist of a Senate and House of Representatives.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 23. Amend § 2, Article II of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2. Composition of House and Senate; terms of office; districts; election.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2.</span><span> The House of Representatives shall be composed of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">thirty-five</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">35</span><span> members, plus such additional members as shall be provided</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> under</span><span> Section 2A of this Article, who shall be chosen for </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2</span><span> years. The Senate shall be composed of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">twenty-one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">21</span><span> members, who shall be chosen for </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">four</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">4</span><span> years.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">The</span><span style="text-decoration: underline">This</span><span> State is hereby divided into </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">thirty-five</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">35</span><span> Representative Districts. There shall be such additional Representative Districts as shall be provided</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> under</span><span> Section 2A of this Article. From each Representative District there shall be chosen, by the qualified electors thereof, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>Representative.</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> The</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> This</span><span> State is also hereby divided into </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">twenty-one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">21 </span><span>Senatorial Districts, from each of which shall be chosen, by the qualified electors thereof, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>Senator. In New Castle County there shall be </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">seven</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">7 </span><span>Senatorial Districts, numbered from </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1</span><span> to </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">seven</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">7</span><span> inclusive; in Kent County, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">seven</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">7 </span><span>Senatorial Districts, numbered from </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1</span><span> to </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">seven</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">7 </span><span>inclusive; and in Sussex County, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">seven</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">7 </span><span>Senatorial Districts from </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>to </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">seven</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">7 </span><span>inclusive.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>The Representative Districts in New Castle County are and shall be as follows:</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number One. All that portion of the City of Wilmington included within the Second and Fourth Wards, and those parts of the Sixth and Eighth Wards, respectively, lying south of and bounded by the central line of Eighth street.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Two. All that portion of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> city included within the Ninth Ward, and those parts of the Sixth and Eighth Wards, respectively, lying north of and bounded by the central line of Eighth street.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Three. All that portion of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> city included within the Seventh Ward, and that part of the Fifth Ward lying north of and bounded by a straight line including the central line of Eighth street.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Four. All that portion of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> city included within the First and Third Wards, and that part of the Fifth Ward lying south of and bounded by the central line of Eighth street, east of and bounded by the central line of Adams street, and west of and bounded by the central line of Market street.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Five. All that portion of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> city included within the Tenth, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Eleventh</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Eleventh, </span><span>and Twelfth Wards, and that part of the Fifth Ward lying south of and bounded by a straight line including the central line of Eighth street, west of and bounded by the central line of Adams street, and bounded on the west by the westerly boundary line of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> city.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Six. Brandywine Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Seven. Christiana Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Eight. Mill Creek Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Nine. White Clay Creek Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Ten. New Castle Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Eleven. Pencader Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Twelve. Red Lion Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Thirteen. St. Georges Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Fourteen. Appoquinimink Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Fifteen. Blackbird Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>The Representative Districts in Kent County are and shall be as follows:</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number One. Duck Creek Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Two. Little Creek Hundred and the first Election District of East Dover Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Three. Kenton Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Four. West Dover Hundred and all that portion of East Dover Hundred lying next to West Dover Hundred and separate from the rest of East Dover Hundred by the following boundary lines: beginning at the middle of the public road leading from the Horsehead road to Kenton at the point of intersection of Kenton Hundred and East Dover Hundred, thence running along the middle of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> road to the Horsehead road, thence running in a westerly direction along the middle of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> Horsehead road a short distance to a short road leading from the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> Horsehead road to the road from Dover to Hazlettville, known as the Hazlettville road, thence running along the middle of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> short road from the Horsehead road to the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> Hazlettville road, thence running in a westerly direction along the middle of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> Hazlettville road a short distance to the road leading therefrom to Wyoming, thence running along the middle of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> road leading from the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> Hazlettville road to Wyoming to the point of intersection of East Dover Hundred and North Murderkill Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Five. All that portion of East Dover Hundred not included in Districts numbers two and four.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Six. Parts of North Murderkill, South Murderkill and Mispillion Hundreds included within the following boundary lines: beginning at the intersection of the southern line of South Murderkill Hundred with the State of Maryland, thence running along the division line between Mispillion Hundred and South Murderkill Hundred to the public road leading from Whiteleysburg to Harrington, thence running in a southeasterly and easterly direction along the middle of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> public road to the public road leading from Masten's Corner to Vernon, at or near White's Church, thence running in a northeasterly direction along the middle of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> public road leading from Masten's Corner to Vernon, a short distance to the public road leading therefrom to the town of Harrington, being a continuation of the road leading from Whiteleysburg to Harrington, thence running in a southeasterly direction to the intersection of West street in the town of Harrington, thence running in a northerly direction along the middle of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> West street to the middle of Wolcott street in</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> town of Harrington, thence running in an easterly direction along the middle of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> Wolcott street to the middle of Dorman street in</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> town of Harrington, thence running in a northerly direction along the middle of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> Dorman street to Brown's Branch, thence running in an easterly direction with the course of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> Branch to the Delaware Railroad, thence running in a northerly direction along said Delaware Railroad to Beaver Dam Branch in South Murderkill Hundred, thence following the course of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> Beaver Dam Branch in a northwesterly direction to the public road leading from Felton to Whiteleysburg, thence running in a northeasterly direction along the middle of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> public road from Felton to Whiteleysburg to the Owl's Nest road, thence running in a northerly direction along the middle of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the said</span><span> Owl's Nest road to the intersection of the Cowgill road from Woodside to Petersburg, thence running in a northerly direction along the middle of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the said</span><span> Cowgill road to the Reed road running from Woodside to DuPont's school house, thence running in a northwesterly direction along the middle of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the said</span><span> Reed road to DuPont's school house, thence running in a northerly direction along the middle of the public road leading from Willow Grove to Camden, a short distance to Stubb's Corner, thence running in a westerly and northwesterly and westerly direction along the middle of the public road leading from DuPont's school house to the Almshouse to Gray's Corner, thence continuing in a direct westerly line to the southern boundary line of West Dover Hundred, thence following the southern boundary line of West Dover Hundred in a westerly direction to the State of Maryland, thence running in a southerly direction along the eastern boundary line to the State of Maryland to the place of beginning.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Seven. All that portion of North Murderkill Hundred not included in District</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> number six.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Number Six.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Eight. All that portion of South Murderkill Hundred not included in District</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> number six.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Number Six.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Nine. All that portion of Mispillion Hundred not included in District</span><span></span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> number six.</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Number Six.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Ten. Milford Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>The Representative Districts in Sussex County are and shall be as follows:</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number One. Cedar Creek Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Two. All that portion of Nanticoke Hundred which lies north and west of Gravelly Branch, beginning at a point where</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the said</span><span> Gravelly Branch intersects the dividing line between Georgetown and Nanticoke Hundreds and running in a southwesterly course to what was formerly known as Rest's Old Mill, thence along</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> branch to what was formerly known as Collins' Mills, to its mouth being at the head of Middleford Mill Pond; together with North West Fork Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Three. All that portion of Nanticoke Hundred which lies south and east of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> Gravelly Branch, beginning at a point where</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the said</span><span> Gravelly Branch intersects the dividing line between Nanticoke and Georgetown Hundreds, running in a southwesterly course to what was formerly known as Rest's Old Mill, thence along</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> branch to what was formerly known as Collins' Mills, to its mouth at the head of Middleford Mill Pond; together with Seaford Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Four. Broad Creek Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Five. Little Creek Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Six. Dagsboro and Gumboro Hundreds.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Seven. Baltimore Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Eight. Indian River Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Nine. Georgetown Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Ten. Broadkiln and Lewes and Rehoboth Hundreds.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>The Senatorial Districts in New Castle County are and shall be as follows:</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number One. All that portion of the City of Wilmington lying north of and bounded by a straight line including the central line of Eighth street extending from the Delaware River to the westerly boundary of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> city.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Two. All that portion of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> City lying south of and bounded by the straight line aforesaid including the central line of Eighth street.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Three. Brandywine Hundred, together with all that portion of Christiana Hundred lying north of and bounded by the central line of Lancaster Turnpike.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Four. Milk Creek Hundred, together with all that portion of Christiana Hundred lying south of and bounded by the central line of the Lancaster Turnpike.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Five. White Clay Creek Hundred, Red Lion Hundred and New Castle Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Six. Pencader Hundred and St. Georges Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Seven. Appoquinimink Hundred and Blackbird Hundred.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>The Senatorial Districts in Kent County are and shall be as follows:</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number One. The first and second Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Two. The third and fourth Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Three. The fifth and seventh Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Four. The sixth and ninth Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Five. The eighth and tenth Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Six, at Large. The first, second, fifth, seventh and eighth Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Seven, at Large. The third, fourth, sixth, ninth and tenth Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>The Senatorial Districts in Sussex County are and shall be as follows:</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number One. The first and second Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Two. The third and fourth Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Three. The fifth and sixth Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Four. The seventh and eighth Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Five. The ninth and tenth Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Six, at Large. The first, second, third, fourth and fifth Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Number Seven, at Large. The sixth, seventh, eighth, ninth and tenth Representative Districts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>The first Senators elected from the Sixth Senatorial Districts of Kent and Sussex Counties shall serve for a </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2 </span><span>year term only, thereafter their successors shall serve for a full </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">four</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">4 </span><span>year term.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>All territory which shall hereafter be added to and included within the City of Wilmington shall become part of the Representative Districts in New Castle County, as follows:</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>All lying east of a straight line including the central line of Market street, below Eighth street, as the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said two</span><span> streets now exist, and south of a straight line including the central line of Eighth street, as the same now exists, shall become part of Representative District</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> number one.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Number One.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>All lying north of a straight line including the central line of Eighth street, as the same now exists, extending from the northeasterly side of Brandywine Creek to the Delaware River, or north of the Brandywine Creek, westerly from the point of intersection of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> straight line with the northeasterly side of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> Creek, shall become part of Representative District</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> number two.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Number Two.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>All lying north of a straight line including the central line of Eighth street, as the same now exists, south of the Brandywine Creek, and west of the central line of Market street, as the same now exists, shall become part of Representative District</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> number three.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Number Three.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>All lying between a straight line including the central line of Market street extended southerly and a straight line including the central line of Washington street extended southerly shall become part of Representative District</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> number four.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Number Four.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>All lying south of a straight line including the central line of Eighth street, as the same now exists, and west of a straight line including the central line of Washington street, as the same now exists, shall become part of Representative District</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> number five.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Number Five.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>In case of any change in the boundary line between this State and the State of Pennsylvania any of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> Senatorial and Representative Districts in New Castle County affected thereby shall conform to any new boundary line between the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> States.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>All territory which shall hereafter be added to and included within the City of Wilmington shall become part of the Senatorial Districts in New Castle County as follows:</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>All lying north of a straight line including the central line of Eighth street, extended from the Delaware River westwardly, shall become part of Senatorial District</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> number one.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Number One.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>All lying south of a straight line including the central line of Eighth street, extended from the Delaware River westwardly shall become part of Senatorial District</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> number two.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Number Two.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Whenever by the extension of the limits of the City of Wilmington territory forming part of any Representative or Senatorial District, as hereby established, shall be included within the limits of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> city, such Representative or Senatorial District shall thereafter consist of the residue thereof, not so included within</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> limits.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>The several Representative and Senatorial Districts in</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State shall, except as herein otherwise provided, continue to be bounded, described and defined by the lines of the hundreds, wards, election districts, public roads, railroad and other boundaries herein mentioned, as the same are now established and located.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 24. Amend § 2A, Article II of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2A. Additional representative districts.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2A.</span><span> In addition to the existing 35 Representative Districts as set forth in Section 2 of this Article, there shall be additional Representative Districts as hereafter provided.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Each existing Representative District as set forth in Section 2 of this Article, with a population residing therein in excess of 15,000, as shown by the last official federal decennial census shall be entitled to </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>additional Representative for each additional 15,000 population or major fraction thereof residing within the District.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Upon any Representative District, as set forth in Section 2 of this Article, being entitled to more than </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>Representative, it shall be subdivided into new Representative Districts for each additional Representative to which it is entitled, from which shall be chosen by the qualified electors thereof, a Representative.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>After each official federal decennial census the new Representative Districts created</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> under</span><span> this Section shall be abolished and the Representative Districts set forth in Section 2 of this Article shall again be re-divided as set forth herein.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>The sub-dividing of the Representative Districts as set forth herein shall be done by a Redistricting Commission, consisting of the Governor, as Chairman, and the State Chairman of the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2 </span><span>political parties receiving the largest vote for Governor at the preceding election for Governor as advisors to the Governor. Redistricting and reapportioning by the Commission as set forth herein shall be accomplished in accordance with the following criteria: Each new Representative District shall, insofar as is possible, be formed of contiguous territory; shall be as nearly equal in population as possible to the other new districts being created within the existing Representative District; shall be bounded by ancient boundaries, major roads, streams, or other natural boundaries; and not be so created as to unduly favor any person or political party.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Within 120 calendar days following the official reporting to the President of the United States of each decennial census, (or within 120 calendar days after this amendment takes effect) the Governor, on behalf of the Commission, shall file with the Secretary of State the plan for redistricting and reapportioning as provided for herein. Forthwith, after the filing, the Governor shall issue a proclamation of redistricting and reapportioning. The Secretary of State shall cause such proclamation to be published in </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2 </span><span>newspapers of general circulation within</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State for </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2 </span><span>consecutive weeks, within 20 days after the issuance of the proclamation. The proclamation shall become effective within 30 days of its issuance.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Any qualified voter may apply to the Superior Court to compel the Governor, by mandamus or otherwise, to perform the redistricting and reapportioning duties or to correct any error in redistricting and reapportioning. Application to compel the Governor to perform the redistricting and reapportioning duties must be filed within </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">thirty</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">30</span><span> days of the expiration of the 120 days allotted to the Commission to file its plan, if such plan is not timely filed. Application to compel correction of any error in redistricting and reapportioning must be filed within </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">thirty</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">30 </span><span>days following the proclamation. Original jurisdiction in these matters is hereby vested in the Superior Court. On appeal, the cause shall be reviewed by the Supreme Court upon the law and the facts.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 25. Amend § 2B, Article II of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2B. Delegates to Constitutional Convention.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2B.</span><span> The number of delegates and the method of electing delegates to the Constitutional Convention as provided in Section 2, Article 16, shall not be effected by the addition of Representatives or Representative Districts,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> under</span><span> Section 2A of this Article. The Representative Districts which shall elect delegates to the Constitutional Convention are as set forth in Section 2 of this Article.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 26. Amend § 3, Article II of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 3. Qualifications of members; commencement of terms of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">members.</span><span></span><span></span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">members; continuing residency required.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 3.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(a) No person shall be a Senator who shall not have attained the age of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">twenty-seven</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">27</span><span> years and have been a citizen and inhabitant of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">three</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">3</span><span> years next preceding the day of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the person’s</span><span> election and the last year of that term an inhabitant of the Senatorial District in which</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> he or she</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the person</span><span> shall be chosen, unless</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> he or she shall have been</span><span> absent on the public business of the United States or of this State. No person shall be a Representative who shall not have attained the age of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">twenty-four</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">24</span><span> years, and have been a citizen and inhabitant of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">three</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">3</span><span> years next preceding the day of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">his or her</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the person’s</span><span> election, and the last year of that term an inhabitant of the Representative District in which</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">he or she</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the person</span><span> shall be chosen, unless</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> he or she shall have been</span><span> absent on the public business of the United States or of this State.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(b) A Senator shall continuously reside in the Senatorial District in which the Senator was chosen during the Senator’s term of office. A Representative shall continuously reside in the Representative District in which the Representative was chosen during the Representative’s term of office. A Senator or Representative who does not continuously reside in the District in which the Senator or Representative was chosen is deemed to have resigned the office.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">(b) The terms of Senators and Representatives begin on the day next after their election.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(c) If, as a result of legislative redistricting, a sitting Senator or Representative is required to change the Senator’s or Representative’s residence in order to maintain residency in the district in which the Senator or Representative represent, subsection (b) of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section </span><span>does not apply.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(d) If, by reason of an event that can be neither anticipated nor controlled, a Senator or Representative is unable to continue to maintain residency in their district, subsection (b) </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">of this Section</span><span> does not apply.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(e) The terms of Senators and Representatives begin on the day next after their election.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 27. Amend § 4, Article II of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 4. Time and frequency of sessions.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(a)</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Section 4. </span><span>The General Assembly shall convene on the second Tuesday of January of each calendar year unless otherwise convened by the Governor, or by mutual call of the presiding officers of both Houses.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(b)</span><span> The General Assembly may continue in session each calendar year so long as, in its judgment, the public interest may require; however, each session may not extend beyond 5:00 p.m. on the last day of June unless the session is recalled by the Governor or the mutual call of the presiding officers of both Houses.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 28. Amend § 5, Article II of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 5. Place of meeting.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 5. </span><span>The General Assembly shall meet and sit in Dover, the capital of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State; provided, however, that in periods of emergency resulting from enemy attack, terrorism, disease, accident, or other natural or man-made disaster the General Assembly may temporarily meet and sit elsewhere.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 29. Amend § 6, Article II of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 6. Vacancies; tenure of office of persons elected to fill.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 6. </span><span>Whenever there shall be a vacancy in either House of the General Assembly, by reason of failure to elect, ineligibility, death, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">resignation</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">resignation,</span><span> or otherwise, a writ of election shall be issued by the presiding officer of the House in which the vacancy exists, or in case of necessity in such other manner as shall be provided by law; and the person thereupon chosen to fill such vacancy shall hold office for the residue of the term. And whenever there shall be such vacancy in either House, and the General Assembly is not in session, the Governor shall have power to issue a writ of election to fill such vacancy, which writ shall be executed as a writ issued by the presiding officer of either House in case of vacancy, and the person thereupon chosen to fill such vacancy shall hold office for the residue of the term.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 30. Amend § 7, Article II of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 7. President pro tempore, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Speaker</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Speaker,</span><span> and other officers; absence of presiding officers.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 7.</span><span> The Senate at the first annual session of every new General Assembly shall choose </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>of its members president pro tempore, who shall preside in the absence of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Lieutenant-Governor,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Lieutenant Governor,</span><span> or in case the latter shall become Governor or while</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> he or she continues</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> continuing</span><span> in the exercise of the office of Governor by reason of disability of the Governor.
