<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(d) If a document or item required under subsection (a) or (b) of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section</span><span> is not produced, or if there is a complaint, under oath, of fraud or mistake in a document or item, or if fraud or mistake is evident on the face of a document completed by the election officers, the Court may do any of the following:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-left: 18pt; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(1) Issue summary process against an election officer, or any other person, to bring the election officer, or person, forthwith into Court with the election papers in their possession or control.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-left: 18pt; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(2) Open the absentee ballot boxes and take there from any paper contained in the absentee ballot boxes.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-left: 18pt; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(3) Recount the absentee ballots.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-left: 18pt; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(4) Correct any fraud or mistake on the voting machine recording tapes or on any document completed by the election officers relating to the election.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(e) The Court has all the other jurisdiction and powers now vested by law in the boards of canvass, and such other powers as provided by law.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(f) After the state of the election is ascertained under this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section,</span><span> the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">the</span><span> Court shall make certificates of the election, under the seal of the Court in the form required by law, and transmit, deliver, and lodge the certificates of the election as required by this Constitution or by law, and deliver the ballot boxes to the sheriff of the county, to be kept and delivered by the sheriff as required by law.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(g) No act or determination of the Court in the discharge of the duties imposed on the Court by this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section </span><span>shall be conclusive in the trial of any contested election.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(h) For the purposes of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section,</span><span> the Superior Court shall consist in New Castle County of the President Judge and resident Judge; in Kent County of the resident Judge and a Judge designated by the President Judge; and in Sussex County of the resident Judge and a Judge designated by the President Judge.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(i) Two Judges constitute a quorum. The Governor may commission a Judge for the purpose of constituting a quorum when by reason of legal exception to any Judge, or for any other cause, a quorum could not otherwise be had.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 127. Amend § 7, Article V of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 7. Election offenses; penalties; self-incrimination.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 7. </span><span>Every person who either in or out of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State shall receive or accept, or offer to receive or accept, or shall pay, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">transfer</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">transfer, </span><span>or deliver, or offer or promise to pay, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">transfer</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">transfer,</span><span> or deliver, or shall contribute, or offer or promise to contribute, to another to be paid or used, any money or other valuable thing as a compensation, inducement or reward for the giving or withholding, or in any manner influencing the giving or withholding, a vote at any general, special, or municipal election in this State, or at any primary election, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">convention</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">convention,</span><span> or meeting held for the purpose of nominating any candidate or candidates to be voted for at such general, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">special</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">special,</span><span> or municipal election; or who either in or out of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State shall make or become directly or indirectly a party to any bet or wager depending upon the result of any such general, special, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">municipal</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">municipal,</span><span> or primary election or convention or meeting, or upon a vote thereat by any person; or who either in or out of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State shall, by the use or promise of money or other valuable thing, or otherwise, cause or attempt to cause any officer of election or registration officer to violate</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> that</span><span> person's official duty; or who either in or out of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State shall by the use or promise of money or other valuable thing influence or attempt to influence any person to be registered or abstain from being registered; or who, being an officer of election or registration officer, shall knowingly and wilfully violate</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> that</span><span> person's official duty; or who shall by force, threat, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">menace</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">menace, </span><span>or intimidation, prevent or hinder, or attempt to prevent or hinder, any person qualified for registration from being registered or any person qualified to vote from voting according to </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">the </span><span>person's choice at any such general, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">special</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">special, </span><span>or municipal election, shall be deemed guilty of a misdemeanor, and shall be fined not less than </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one hundred dollars</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">$100 </span><span>nor more than </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">five thousand dollars,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">$5,000, </span><span>or shall be imprisoned for a term not less than </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1</span><span> month nor more than </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">three</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">3</span><span> years, or shall suffer both fine and imprisonment within</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> those</span><span> limits, at the discretion of the court; and shall further for a term of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">ten</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">10</span><span> years next following </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">the</span><span> person's sentence, be incapable of voting at any such general, special, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">municipal</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">municipal, </span><span>or primary election or convention or meeting; but the penalty of disfranchisement shall not apply to any person making or being a party to any bet or wager, depending upon the result of any such general, special,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> municipal</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">municipal, </span><span>or primary election or convention or meeting. Every person charged with the commission while out of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State of any of the offenses enumerated in this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section,</span><span> and by this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section</span><span> made punishable, whether committed in or out of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State, may be prosecuted under Section 8 of this Article in any county in which</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> person shall be arrested on such charge. No person, other than the accused, shall, in the prosecution for any offense mentioned in this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section,</span><span> be permitted to withhold</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> that</span><span> person's </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">own </span><span>testimony on the ground that it may criminate</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said person</span><span> or subject</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> person to public infamy; but such testimony shall not afterwards be used against</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> person in any judicial proceeding, except for perjury in giving such testimony.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 128. Amend § 8, Article V of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 8. Prosecution for election offenses; procedure; appeal; bond.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 8. </span><span>Every prosecution for any of the offenses mentioned in Section 7 of this Article shall be on information filed by the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Attorney-General,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Attorney General</span><span> after examination and commitment or holding to bail by a judge or Justice of the Peace, and the cause shall be heard,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> tried</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> tried,</span><span> and determined by the court without the intervention of either a grand jury or petit jury. The</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> accused</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> accused,</span><span> if adjudged guilty of the offense</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> charged against him or her,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> charged,</span><span> shall have the right at any time within the space of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">three</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">3 </span><span>calendar months next after sentence is pronounced to an appeal to the Supreme Court. The court below, or any judge thereof, in term time or vacation, shall upon application by the accused allow such appeal; but such appeal shall not operate as a supersedeas unless the appellant shall at the time of the allowance thereof give an appeal bond to the State of Delaware in such amount and with such surety as shall be approved by such court or judge. On such appeal the Supreme Court shall, with all convenient speed, review the evidence adduced in the cause in the court below, as well as the other proceedings therein, and the law applicable thereto, and give final judgment accordingly, either affirming or reversing the judgment below. If the appellant shall fail to prosecute</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> appeal</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> under</span><span> the rules and practice hereinafter provided for, the Supreme Court shall affirm the judgment of the court below. Where the sentence in the court below includes a term of imprisonment and an appeal bond is given and approved in manner aforesaid, the Supreme Court, if it affirm the judgment below, shall sentence the appellant to a term of imprisonment equal to that imposed by the court below, after deducting therefrom a period equal to the time of imprisonment, if any, already suffered </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">by him or her</span><span> under the sentence of the court below. The surety or sureties in any appeal bond given under the provisions of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section </span><span>shall have the right at any time after its approval and until final judgment shall be rendered by the Supreme Court, and, in case the judgment of the court below shall be affirmed, until the expiration of the space of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">thirty</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">30 </span><span>days next following such affirmance, to take, wherever found, and render the appellant to the sheriff of the county in which</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> he or she</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the appellant</span><span> was sentenced; and a certified copy of the appeal bond shall be the sufficient warrant for such surety or sureties for such taking and rendering. If the Supreme Court shall reverse any judgment of the court below imposing a fine, and if the accused shall have fully paid such fine and the costs of prosecution, the amount thereof shall be refunded to the appellant through a warrant drawn by the court below on the treasurer of the county in which the accused was sentenced. All the judges entitled to sit in the Supreme Court shall, as soon as conveniently may be, meet at the usual place of sitting of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> court, and they, or a majority of them, shall adopt rules prescribing the forms and conditions of appeal bonds to be used under the provisions of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section, </span><span>and the manner of certifying copies thereof, providing for the printing or reduction to writing of all oral evidence in the cause in the court below and of the opinion of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> court, for the certification of the same when so printed or reduced to writing, and of copies thereof; for the copying and certification of all documentary or other written or printed evidence in the cause in the court below and of the record therein; for the transmission to the Supreme Court of such certified copies of such record, and of all the evidence adduced in the court below and of the opinion of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> court for the transmission to the court below of a certified copy of the final judgment of the Supreme Court and of any additional sentence pronounced by</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> court, for the discharge of securities in appeal bonds, and for the framing, issuance,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> service</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> service,</span><span> and enforcement of all process and rules necessary to give full effect to the provisions of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section;</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section;</span><span> and regulating generally the practice and procedure of the Supreme Court and the court below in cases of appeal under this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section.</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section. </span><span>The</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> judges, or a majority of them, met as aforesaid, may also provide that when complaint shall be made in due form, prescribed by them, to any judge entitled to sit in the Supreme Court, that any offense mentioned in Section 7 of this Article has been committed in the county in which such judge shall reside, or out of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State, such judge shall have power to cause the person charged with such offense to be arrested within any county of this State and brought before</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> him or her,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> such judge,</span><span> and to bind</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> him or her</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the person</span><span> with sufficient surety, or, for want of bail, commit</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> him or her</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the person</span><span> for</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span> appearance and answer at the next term of the Court of General Sessions in such manner and under and</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to</span><span> such rules and regulations as the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> judges, or a majority of them, shall prescribe.</span><span> From time to time hereafter, whenever a majority of all the judges entitled to sit in the Supreme Court shall so request, all of the judges so entitled shall, as soon as conveniently may be, meet at the usual place of sitting of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> court; and they, or a majority of them, shall have power to revise, amend, add</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> to</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> to,</span><span> or annul, any rule or rules theretofore adopted touching forms, practice or procedure in cases of appeal under this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section, </span><span>or arrest and binding or commitment for appearance and answer, in such manner and to such extent as in their judgment shall best serve to effectuate the purposes hereof. No person shall be adjudged guilty of an offense mentioned in Section 7 of this Article without the concurrence of all the judges trying the case; and upon appeal no judgment of the court below shall be affirmed without the concurrence of all of the judges of the Supreme Court sitting in the case, and their failure to concur as aforesaid shall operate as a reversal of the judgment of the court below; provided, however, that such concurrence of the judges sitting in the Supreme Court shall not be necessary for the affirmance of the judgment of the court below where the appellant shall fail to prosecute</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> appeal</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> under</span><span> the rules and practices herein provided for.</span><span></span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 129. Amend § 9, Article V of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 9. Enumeration of election offenses as limitation on power of General Assembly.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 9. </span><span>The enumeration of the offenses mentioned in Section 7 of this Article shall not preclude the General Assembly from defining and providing for the punishment of other offenses against the freedom and purity of the ballot, or touching the conduct,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> returns</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> returns,</span><span> or ascertainment of the result of general,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> special</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> special,</span><span> or municipal elections, or of primary elections,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> conventions</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> conventions,</span><span> or meetings held for the nomination of candidates to be voted for at general,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> special</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> special,</span><span> or municipal elections. No prosecution under any</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> act</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Act</span><span> of the General Assembly passed </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">pursuant to</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">under</span><span> this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section </span><span>shall be subject to the provisions of Section 8 of this Article.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 130. Amend § 1, Article VI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. Impeachment power of House; trial by Senate; oath of Senators; vote; presiding officers.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(a)</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 1. </span><span>The House of Representatives shall have the sole power of impeaching; but two-thirds of all the members must concur in an impeachment. All impeachments shall be tried by the Senate, and when sitting for that purpose, the Senators shall be upon oath or affirmation to do justice according to the evidence. No person shall be convicted without the concurrence of two-thirds of all the Senators.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(b)</span><span> On the trial of an impeachment against the Governor or</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Lieutenant-Governor,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Lieutenant Governor,</span><span> the Chief Justice, or, in case of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span> absence or disability, the Chancellor shall preside; and on the trial of all other impeachments the President of the Senate shall preside.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 131. Amend § 2, Article VI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2. Grounds for impeachment.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2. </span><span>The Governor and all other civil officers under this State shall be liable to impeachment for treason, bribery, or any high crime or misdemeanor in office. Judgment in such cases shall not extend further than to removal from office, and disqualification to hold any office of honor,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> trust</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> trust,</span><span> or profit, under this State; but the party convicted shall, nevertheless, be subject to indictment, trial, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">judgment</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">judgment,</span><span> and punishment according to law.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 132. Amend § 3, Article VI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 3. Treason.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 3. </span><span>Treason against this State shall consist only in levying war against it, or in adhering to the enemies of the Government, giving them aid and comfort. No person shall be convicted of treason unless on the testimony of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2 </span><span>witnesses to the same overt act or on confession in open court.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 133. Amend § 1, Article VII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. Power of Governor; recommendation of Board of Pardons; entry in register and submission to General Assembly.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(a)</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 1. </span><span>The Governor shall have power to remit fines and forfeitures and to grant reprieves, commutations of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">sentence</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">sentence,</span><span> and pardons, except in cases of impeachment; but no pardon, or reprieve for more than </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">six</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">6</span><span> months, shall be granted, nor sentence commuted, except upon the recommendation in writing of a majority of the Board of Pardons after full hearing; and such recommendation, with the reasons therefor at length, shall be filed and recorded in the office of the Secretary of State, who shall forthwith notify the Governor thereof.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(b)</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">He or she</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">The Governor</span><span> shall fully set forth in writing the grounds of all reprieves,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pardons</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> pardons,</span><span> and remissions, to be entered in the register of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">the Governor’s </span><span>official acts and laid before the General Assembly at </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">its</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">the General Assembly’s </span><span>next session.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 134. Amend § 2, Article VII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2. Composition of Board of Pardons.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2. </span><span>The Board of Pardons shall be composed of the Chancellor,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Lieutenant-Governor,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Lieutenant Governor,</span><span> Secretary of State, State</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Treasurer</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Treasurer,</span><span> and Auditor of Accounts.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 135. Amend § 3, Article VII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 3. Information from Attorney General on Board's duties.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 3.</span><span> The </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said board</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Board of Pardons</span><span> may require information from the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Attorney-General</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Attorney General</span><span> upon any subject relating to the duties of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said board.</span><span></span><span></span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">the Board.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 136. Amend § 1, Article VIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. Uniformity of taxes; collection under general laws; exemption for public welfare purposes.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(a)</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 1.</span><span> All taxes shall be uniform upon the same class of subjects within the territorial limits of the authority levying the tax, except as otherwise permitted herein, and shall be levied and collected under general laws passed by the General Assembly. County Councils of New Castle and Sussex Counties and the Levy Court of Kent County are hereby authorized to exempt from county taxation such property in their respective counties as in their opinion will best promote the public welfare. The county property tax exemption power created by this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section </span><span>shall be exclusive as to such property as is located within the respective counties. With respect to real property located within the boundaries of any incorporated municipality, the authority to exempt such property from municipal property tax shall be exercised by the respective incorporated municipality, when in the opinion of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> municipality it will best promote the public welfare.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(b)</span><span> The </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">legislature</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">General Assembly</span><span> shall enact laws to provide that the value of land which is determined by the assessing officer of the taxing jurisdiction to be actively devoted to agriculture use and to have been so devoted for at least the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2 </span><span>successive years immediately preceding the tax year in issue, shall, for local tax purposes, on application of the owner, be that value which such land has for agricultural use.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(c)</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Any such laws</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">A law enacted under subsection (b) of this Section </span><span>shall provide that when land which has been valued in this manner for local tax purposes is applied to a use other than for agriculture, it shall be subject to additional taxes in an amount equal to the difference, if any, between the taxes paid or payable on the basis of the valuation and the assessment authorized hereunder and the taxes that would have been paid or payable had the land been valued and assessed as otherwise provided in this Constitution, in the current year and in such of the tax years immediately preceding, not less than </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2 </span><span>such years in which the land was valued as herein authorized.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(d)</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Such laws</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">A law enacted under subsection (b) of this Section </span><span>shall also provide for the assessment and collection of any additional taxes levied thereupon and shall include such other provisions as shall be necessary to carry out </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">the provisions of this amendment.</span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this Section.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 137. Amend § 2, Article VIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2. Revenue bills to originate in House; amendments by Senate; restriction on definition; exclusion of unrelated matter.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2. </span><span>All bills for raising revenue shall originate in the House of Representatives; but the Senate may propose alterations as on other bills; and no bill from the operation of which, when passed into a law, revenue may incidentally arise shall be accounted a bill for raising revenue; nor shall any matter or cause whatever not immediately relating to and necessary for raising revenue be in any manner blended with or annexed to a bill for raising revenue.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 138. Amend § 3, Article VIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 3. Borrowing money; specification of purpose; surplus borrowed money.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 3. </span><span>No money shall be borrowed or debt created by or on behalf of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State but</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> by</span><span> an Act of the General Assembly, passed with the concurrence of three-fourths of all the members elected to each House, except to supply casual deficiencies of revenue, repel invasion, suppress insurrection, defend</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State in war, or pay existing debts; and any law authorizing the borrowing of money by or on behalf of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State shall specify the purpose for which the money is to be borrowed, and the money so borrowed shall be used exclusively for such purpose; but should the money so borrowed or any part thereof be left after the abandonment of such purpose or the accomplishment thereof, such money, or the surplus thereof, may be disposed of according to law.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 139. Amend § 4, Article VIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 4. Restrictions on loan of public money or bonds and credit of State.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 4. </span><span>No appropriation of the public money shall be made to, nor the bonds of this State be issued or loaned to any county, municipality, or corporation, nor shall the credit of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State, by the guarantee or the endorsement of the bonds or other undertakings of any county, municipality, or corporation, be pledged otherwise than</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> by</span><span> an Act of the General Assembly, passed with the concurrence of three-fourths of all the members elected to each House.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 140. Amend § 5, Article VIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 5. Capitation tax; uniformity; use.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 5. </span><span>The General Assembly shall provide for levying and collecting a capitation tax from every citizen of the State of the age of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">twenty-one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">21</span><span> years or upwards; but such tax to be collected in any County shall be uniform throughout that County, and such capitation tax shall be used exclusively in the County in which it is collected.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 141. Amend § 6, Article VIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 6. Procedure in withdrawal and payment of public moneys; annual publication of receipts and expenditures; limitation upon appropriations.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 6.</span><span> (a) No money shall be drawn from the treasury but</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> by</span><span> an appropriation made by Act of the General Assembly; provided, however, that the compensation of the members of the General Assembly and all expenses connected with the session thereof may be paid out of the treasury</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> by</span><span> resolution in that behalf; a regular account of the receipts and expenditures of all public money shall be published annually.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(b) No appropriation, supplemental </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">appropriation</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">appropriation, </span><span>or budget</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> act</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Act</span><span> shall cause the aggregate State General Fund appropriations enacted for any given fiscal year to exceed</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> 98 percent</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> 98%</span><span> of the estimated State General Fund revenue for such fiscal year from all sources, including estimated unencumbered funds remaining at the end of the previous fiscal year. An</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> act</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Act</span><span> approved </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">pursuant to</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">§ 3 of this article</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">under Section 3 of this Article </span><span>shall not be considered an appropriation for the purpose of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section.</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section.</span><span> Estimated unencumbered funds are calculated by taking the estimated General Fund cash balance at the end of the fiscal year less estimated revenue anticipation bonds or notes, estimated encumbrances, estimated continuing</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> appropriations</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> appropriations,</span><span> and the amount of the Budget Reserve Account as established in subsection (d) of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section </span><span>at the end of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">the </span><span>fiscal year. The amount of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">the</span><span> revenue estimate and estimated unencumbered funds remaining shall be determined by the most recent joint resolution approved from time to time by a majority of the members elected to each House of the General Assembly and signed by the Governor.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(c) Notwithstanding subsection (b) of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section, </span><span>any portion of the amount between</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> 98 and 100 percent</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> 98% and 100%</span><span> of the estimated State General Fund revenue for any fiscal year as estimated in accordance with subsection (b) of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section </span><span>may be appropriated in any given fiscal year in the event of emergencies involving the health,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> safety</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> safety,</span><span> or welfare of the citizens of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State, such appropriations to be approved by three-fifths of the members elected to each House of the General Assembly.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(d) There is hereby established a Budget Reserve Account within the General Fund. Within 45 days after the end of any fiscal year, the excess of any unencumbered funds remaining from the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> fiscal year shall be paid into the Budget Reserve Account, provided, however, that no such payment will be made which would increase the total of the Budget Reserve Account to more than</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> 5 percent</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> 5%</span><span> of only the estimated State General Fund revenues as set by subsection (b) of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section.</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section. </span><span>The excess of any unencumbered funds shall be determined by subtracting from the actual unencumbered funds at the end of any fiscal year an amount which together with the latest estimated revenues is necessary to fund the ensuing fiscal year's General Fund budget including the required estimated General Fund supplemental and automatic appropriations for</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> ensuing fiscal year less estimated reversions. The General </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Assembly</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Assembly, </span><span>by a three-fifths vote of the members elected to each House, may appropriate from the Budget Reserve Account such additional sums as may be necessary to fund any unanticipated deficit in any given fiscal year or to provide funds required as a result of any revenue reduction enacted by the General Assembly.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 142. Amend § 7, Article VIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 7. Real estate assessments; inclusion of values.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 7.</span><span> In all assessments of the value of real estate for taxation, the value of the land and the value of the buildings and improvements thereon shall be included. And in all assessments of the rental value of real estate for taxation, the rental value of the land and the rental value of the buildings and the improvements thereon shall be included. The foregoing provisions of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section </span><span>shall apply to all assessments of the value of real estate or of the rental value thereof for taxation for State, county, hundred, school,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> municipal</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> municipal,</span><span> or other public purposes.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 143. Amend § 8, Article VIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 8. Lending credit, appropriating money </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">to</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">to,</span><span> or </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">becoming interested</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">investing</span><span> in any private corporation, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">person</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">person, </span><span>or company by county or municipality.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 8.</span><span> No county, city,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> town</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> town,</span><span> or other municipality shall lend its credit or appropriate money to, or assume the debt of, or become a shareholder or joint owner in or with any private corporation or any person or company whatever.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 144. Amend § 9, Article VIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 9. Retroactive increase of taxation of personal income.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Any law which shall have the effect of increasing the rates of taxation on personal income for any year or part thereof </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">prior to</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">before</span><span> the date of the enactment thereof, or for any year or years </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">prior to</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">before</span><span> the year in which the law is enacted, shall be void.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 145. Amend § 10, Article VIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 10. Limitation on increase of rate of taxes and license fees; exception to meet obligation under faith and credit pledge; allocation of public moneys to meet such obligation if revenues are not sufficient to meet such pledge.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 10.</span><span> (a) The effective rate of any tax levied or license fee imposed by</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State may not be increased except</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> by an Act</span><span> an act of the General Assembly adopted with the concurrence of three-fifths of all members of each House.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(b) </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Prior to</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Before</span><span> the beginning of each fiscal year of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State, the General Assembly shall appropriate revenues of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State to pay interest on its debt to which it has pledged its faith and credit and which interest is payable in the year for which such appropriation is made and to pay the principal of such debt, payable in such year, whether at maturity or otherwise. To the extent that insufficient revenues of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State are available to pay principal of and interest on such debt when due and payable, the first public moneys of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State thereafter received shall be set aside and applied to the payment of the principal of and interest on such debt. To make up for such insufficient revenues, the General Assembly may increase the rate of taxes and fees without regard to the limitations of subsection (a) </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">hereof</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">of this Section</span><span> after the failure to pay when due the principal of and interest on such debt.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 146. Amend § 11, Article VIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 11. Imposition or levy of new taxes or license fees.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 11.</span><span> (a) No tax or license fee may be imposed or levied except</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to an act</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> by an Act</span><span> of the General Assembly adopted with the concurrence of three-fifths of all members of each House.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(b) </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Prior to</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Before</span><span> the beginning of each fiscal year of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State, the General Assembly shall appropriate revenues of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State to pay interest on its debt to which it has pledged its faith and credit and which interest is payable in the year for which such appropriation is made and to pay the principal of such debt, payable in such year, whether at maturity or otherwise. To the extent that insufficient revenues of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State are available to pay principal of and interest on such debt when due and payable, the first public moneys of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State thereafter received shall be set aside and applied to the payment of the principal of and interest on such debt. To make up for such insufficient revenues, the General Assembly may increase the rate of taxes and fees without regard to the limitations of subsection (a) </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">hereof</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">of this Section </span><span>after the failure to pay when due the principal of and interest on such debt.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">(c)</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">This amendment shall not apply to any tax or license fee authorized by an act of the General Assembly but not effective upon the effective date of this amendment.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 147. Amend § 12, Article VIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 12. The Transportation Trust Fund; use and restrictions.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(a) </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">The</span><span style="text-decoration: underline">This</span><span> State irrevocably pledges and assigns and continuously appropriates the proceeds derived from a motor vehicle registration fee, a motor vehicle document fee, a motor fuel tax, a motor carrier road use</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> tax</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> tax,</span><span> and registration fee, and the operation of the Delaware Turnpike to a special fund known as the Transportation Trust Fund.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(b) The moneys in the Transportation Trust Fund may be appropriated and used for the following purposes:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-left: 18pt; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(1) Capital expenditures on the public transportation system, including the road system,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> grants</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> grants,</span><span> and allocations for investments in transportation, the transit system, and the support systems for public transportation.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-left: 18pt; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(2) Payment of the interest and principal on all indebtedness incurred before or after </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">the effective date of this Act,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">May 18, 2017, </span><span>including the payment of all other obligations incurred</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> pursuant to</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> under</span><span> any trust agreement related to such indebtedness, and secured by moneys in the Transportation Trust Fund.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-left: 18pt; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(3) Other transportation-related purposes, including operating expenses, to which moneys in the Transportation Trust Fund are authorized on </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">the effective date of this Act.</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">May 18, 2017.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(c) No moneys in the Transportation Trust Fund may be appropriated for a purpose not listed in subsection (b) of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section </span><span>except by an</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> act</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Act</span><span> of the General Assembly adopted with the concurrence of three-fourths of all members of each House and separate from an annual budget</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> act,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Act,</span><span> bond and capital improvement</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> act,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Act,</span><span> or grants-in-aid</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> act.</span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Act.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>(d) If moneys in the Transportation Trust Fund cease to be appropriated for a purpose under paragraph (b)(3) of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section, </span><span>the moneys may not again be appropriated for a purpose under paragraph (b)(3) of this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section </span><span>except by an</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> act</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Act</span><span> of the General Assembly adopted with the concurrence of three-fourths of all members of each House and separate from an annual budget</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> act,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Act,</span><span> bond and capital improvement</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> act,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Act,</span><span> or grants-in-aid</span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> act,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Act.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 148. Amend § 1, Article IX of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. Creation, amendment,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> renewal</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> renewal,</span><span> or revival by general law; exceptions; revocation or forfeitures of charters; requisites for enactment of corporation laws.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 1.</span><span> No corporation shall hereafter be created, amended,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> renewed</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> renewed,</span><span> or revived by special act, but only by or under general law, nor shall any existing corporate charter be amended,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> renewed</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> renewed,</span><span> or revived by special act, but only by or under general law; but the foregoing provisions shall not apply to municipal corporations,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> banks</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> banks,</span><span> or corporations for charitable, penal, reformatory, or educational purposes, sustained in whole or in part by</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State. The General Assembly shall, by general law, provide for the revocation or forfeiture of the charters of all corporations for the abuse, misuse, or </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">non-user</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">nonuse</span><span> of their corporate powers,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> privileges</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> privileges,</span><span> or franchises. Any proceeding for such revocation or forfeiture, shall be taken by the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Attorney-General,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Attorney General,</span><span> as may be provided by law. No general incorporation law, nor any special act of incorporation, shall be enacted without the concurrence of two-thirds of all the members elected to each House of the General Assembly.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 149. Amend § 2, Article IX of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2. Acceptance of Constitution by existing corporations as prerequisite for amendment or renewal of charter.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2.</span><span> No corporation in existence </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">at the adoption of this Constitution</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">on June 10, 1897, </span><span>shall have </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">it’s the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> corporation’s</span><span> charter amended or renewed without first filing, under the corporate seal of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> corporation, and duly attested, in the office of the Secretary of State, an acceptance of the provisions of this Constitution.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 150. Amend § 3, Article IX of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 3. Issuance of stock.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">[</span><span>Repealed 74 Del. Laws, c. 281, June 30,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> 2004</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2004.]</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 151. Amend § 4, Article IX of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 4. Rights, privileges,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> immunities</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> immunities,</span><span> and estates.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 4.</span><span> The rights, privileges,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> immunities</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> immunities,</span><span> and estates of religious societies and corporate bodies, except as herein otherwise provided, shall remain as if the Constitution of this State had not been altered.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 152. Amend § 5, Article IX of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 5. Designation, by foreign corporation, of agent for service of process.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 5.</span><span> No foreign corporation shall do any business in this State through or by branch offices,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> agents</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> agents,</span><span> or representatives located in this State, without having an authorized agent or agents in</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State upon whom legal process may be served.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 153. Amend § 6, Article IX of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 6. Taxation of stock owned by persons or corporations without</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 6.</span><span> Shares of the capital stock of corporations created under the laws of this State, when owned by persons or corporations without this State, shall not be subject to taxation by any law now existing or hereafter to be made.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 154. Amend § 1, Article X of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. Establishment and maintenance of free public schools; attendance.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 1.</span><span> The General Assembly shall provide for the establishment and maintenance of a general and efficient system of free public schools, and may require by law that every child, not physically or mentally disabled, shall attend the public school, unless educated by other means.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 155. Amend § 2, Article X of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2. Annual appropriations; apportionment; use of funds; </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">separation of schools;</span><span> other expenses.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2.</span><span> In addition to the income of the investments of the Public School Fund, the General Assembly shall make provision for the annual payment of not less than </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one hundred thousand dollars</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">$100,000 </span><span>for the benefit of the free public schools which, with the income of the investments of the Public School Fund, shall be equitably apportioned among the school districts of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State as the General Assembly shall provide; and the money so apportioned shall be used exclusively for the payment of teachers' salaries and for furnishing free text books; provided, however, that in such apportionment, no distinction shall be made on account of race or color. All other expenses connected with the maintenance of free public schools, and all expenses connected with the erection or repair of free public school buildings shall be defrayed in such manner as shall be provided by law.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 156. Amend § 3, Article X of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
