<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 3. Use of educational funds by religious schools; exemption of school property from taxation.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 3.</span><span> No portion of any fund now existing, or which may hereafter be appropriated, or raised by tax, for educational purposes, shall be appropriated to, or used by, or in aid of any sectarian,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> church</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> church,</span><span> or denominational school; provided, that all real or personal property used for school purposes, where the tuition is free, shall be exempt from taxation and assessment for public purposes.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 157. Amend § 4, Article X of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 4. Use of Public School Fund.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 4.</span><span> No part of the principal or income of the Public School Fund, now or hereafter existing, shall be used for any other purpose than the support of free public schools.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 158. Amend § 5, Article X of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 5. Transportation of nonpublic school students.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 5.</span><span> The General Assembly, notwithstanding any other provision of this Constitution, may provide by an Act of the General Assembly, passed with the concurrence of a majority of all the members elected to each House, for the transportation of students of nonpublic, nonprofit</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Elementary and High Schools.</span><span></span><span></span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">elementary and high schools.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 159. Amend § 6, Article X of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 6. Property tax; use limitations.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 6. </span><span>No property tax receipts received by a public school district as a result of a property tax levied for a particular purpose shall be used for any other purpose except upon the favorable vote of a majority of the eligible voters in the district voting on the question.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 160. Amend § 1, Article XI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. State Board of Agriculture.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 1.</span><span> There shall be a department established and maintained, known as the State Board of Agriculture. </span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 161. Amend § 2, Article XI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2. Composition of Board; residence of Commissioners; quorum.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2.</span><span> The </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said board</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">State Board of Agriculture </span><span>shall be composed of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">three</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">3 </span><span>Commissioners of Agriculture, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>of whom shall reside in each county in</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State. Any </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2 </span><span>of them shall constitute a quorum for the transaction of business. </span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 162. Amend § 3, Article XI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 3. Appointment of Commissioners by Governor; </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">tenure;</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">terms of office; </span><span>vacancies.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 3.</span><span> The </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> Commissioners of Agriculture shall be appointed by the Governor, by and with the consent of a majority of all the members elected to the Senate, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>for the term of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>year, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>for the term of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2 </span><span>years, and </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>for the term of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">three</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">3 </span><span>years; and thereafter all appointments of Commissioners of Agriculture shall be made as aforesaid for the term of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">three</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">3 </span><span>years, and they shall hold office until their successors are duly qualified; provided, that any vacancy occurring in the office of Commissioner of Agriculture before the expiration of a term shall be filled by appointment as aforesaid for the remainder of the term; and provided further, that in case such vacancy shall occur when the Senate is not in session, such vacancy may be filled by the Governor without confirmation by the Senate until the end of the next session of the Senate.</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> </span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 163. Amend § 4, Article XI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 4. Abatement and prevention of diseases of fruit trees, plants, vegetables,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> cereals</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> cereals,</span><span> and livestock.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 4.</span><span> The </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said board</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">State Board of Agriculture </span><span>shall have power to abate and prevent, by such means as the General Assembly shall prescribe, all contagious and infectious diseases of fruit trees, plants, vegetables, cereals, horses, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">cattle</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">cattle, </span><span>and other farm animals.</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> </span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 164. Amend § 5, Article XI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 5. Plans for securing immigration of industrious and useful settlers.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 5.</span><span> The </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> Commissioners </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">of Agriculture </span><span>may devise such plans for securing immigration to this State of industrious and useful settlers as they may deem expedient, and such plans may be executed as prescribed by the General Assembly.</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> </span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 165. Amend § 6, Article XI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 6. Compensation of Board members.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 6.</span><span> The General Assembly shall provide by law for the compensation of the members of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said board.</span><span> </span><span></span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: underline">the State Board of Agriculture.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 166. Amend § 7, Article XI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 7. Duration of Board.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 7.</span><span> The </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">State </span><span>Board of Agriculture hereby established shall continue for </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">eight</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">8 </span><span>years from the date of the qualification of the first member thereof, after which it may be abolished by the General Assembly. </span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 167. Amend § 1, Article XII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. State Board of Health; local boards; powers.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Repealed.</span><span style="text-decoration: underline">[Repealed.]</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 168. Amend § 1, Article XIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. Submission of liquor question to district electors; election.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 1.</span><span> The General Assembly may from time to time provide by law for the submission to the vote of the qualified electors of the several districts of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State, or any of them, mentioned in Section 2 of this Article, the question whether the manufacture and sale of intoxicating liquors shall be licensed or prohibited within the limits thereof; and in every district in which there is a majority against license, no person,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> firm</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> firm,</span><span> or corporation shall thereafter manufacture or sell spirituous,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> vinous</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> vinous,</span><span> or malt liquors, except for medicinal or sacramental purposes, within</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> district, until at a subsequent submission of such question a majority of votes shall be cast in</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> district for license. Whenever a majority of all the members elected to each House of the General Assembly by the qualified electors in any district named in Section 2 of this Article shall request the submission of the question of license or no license to a vote of the qualified electors in</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> district, the General Assembly shall provide for the submission of such question to the qualified electors in such district at the next general election thereafter.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 169. Amend § 2, Article XIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2. Designation of districts for purposes of article.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2.</span><span> Under the provisions of this Article, Sussex County shall comprise </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>district, Kent County </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>district, the City of Wilmington, as its corporate limits now are or may hereafter be extended, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>district, and the remaining part of New Castle County </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>district.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 170. Amend § 3, Article XIII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 3. Laws for enforcement, manufacture and sale, and penalties.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 3.</span><span> The General Assembly shall provide necessary laws to carry out and enforce the provisions of this Article, enact laws governing the manufacture and sale of intoxicating liquors under the limitation of this Article, and provide such penalties as may be necessary to enforce the same.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 171. Amend § 1, Article XIV of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. Form of oath for members of General Assembly and public officers.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(a) </span><span>Members of the General Assembly and all public</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> officers</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> officers,</span><span> executive and judicial, except such inferior officers as shall be by law exempted, shall, before they enter upon the duties of their respective offices, take and subscribe the following oath or affirmation:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>"I, (name), do proudly swear (or affirm) to carry out the responsibilities of the office of (name of office) to the best of my ability, freely acknowledging that the powers of this office flow from the people I am privileged to represent. I further swear (or affirm) always to place the public interests above any special or personal interests, and to respect the right of future generations to share the rich historic and natural heritage of Delaware. In doing so I will always uphold and defend the Constitutions of my Country and my State, so help me God."</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(b)</span><span> No other oath, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">declaration</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">declaration, </span><span>or test shall be required as a qualification for any office of public trust.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 172. Amend § 1, Article XV of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. Conservators of the peace.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 1.</span><span> The Chancellor,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Judges</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Judges,</span><span> and</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Attorney-General</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Attorney General</span><span> shall be conservators of the peace throughout the State; and the Sheriffs shall be conservators of the peace within the counties respectively in which they reside.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 173. Amend § 2, Article XV of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2. Receipt for fees.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2.</span><span> No public officer shall receive any fees without giving to the person paying the same a receipt therefor, if required, therein specifying every item and charge.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 174. Amend § 3, Article XV of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 3. Costs on bill returned ignoramus or on acquittal.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 3. </span><span>No costs shall be paid by a person accused, on a bill returned ignoramus, nor on acquittal.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 175. Amend § 4, Article XV of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 4. Extension of term of public officer; diminution of salary or emoluments.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 4.</span><span> No law shall extend the term of any public officer or diminish the salary or emoluments after</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span> election or appointment. The term "salary or emoluments" as used </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">herein</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">in this Section</span><span> refers to the actual salary or emoluments being provided an officer at any time during</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span> tenure in office and shall not be construed to mean increases in salary or emoluments scheduled by statute for a future date and not yet received by the officer.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 176. Amend § 5, Article XV of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 5. Officers to hold office until successors qualify.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 5.</span><span> All public officers shall hold their respective offices until their successors shall be duly qualified, except in cases herein otherwise provided.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 177. Amend § 6, Article XV of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 6. Behavior of officers; removal for misbehavior or infamous crime.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 6.</span><span> All public officers shall hold their offices on condition that they behave themselves well. The Governor shall remove from office any public officer convicted of misbehavior in office or of any infamous crime.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 178. Amend § 7, Article XV of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 7. Offenses excepted from prohibition against prosecuting by information and jury trial.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 7.</span><span> The matters within Section 28 of Article IV and Sections 7 and 8 of Article V are excepted from the provision of the Constitution that "No person shall for any indictable offense be proceeded against criminally by information," and also from the provisions of the Constitution concerning trial by jury.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 179. Amend § 8, Article XV of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 8. Interest of member or officer of department in contracts for supplies or services of department prohibited.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 8.</span><span> No member or officer of any department of the government shall be in any way interested in any contract for the furnishing of stationery, printing,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> paper</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> paper,</span><span> and fuel used in the legislative and other departments of government; or for the printing,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> binding</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> binding,</span><span> and distributing of the laws, journals, official reports, and all other printing and binding, and the repairing and furnishing the halls and rooms used for the meetings of the General Assembly and its committees, when such contract is awarded to or by any such member,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> officer</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> officer,</span><span> or department.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 180. Amend § 9, Article XV of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 9. Prefixing Constitution to codification of laws.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 9.</span><span> This Constitution shall be prefixed to every codification of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Laws</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> laws</span><span> of this State.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 181. Amend § 10, Article XV of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 10. Disqualification to hold office by reason of sex.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 10.</span><span> No citizen of the State of Delaware shall be disqualified to hold and enjoy any office, or public trust, under the laws of this State, by reason of sex.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 182. Amend § 1, Article XVI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. Proposal and concurrence of Constitutional amendments in General Assembly; procedure.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 1. </span><span>Any amendment or amendments to this Constitution may be proposed in the Senate or House of Representatives; and if the same shall be agreed to by two-thirds of all the members elected to each House, such proposed amendment or amendments shall be entered on their journals, with the yeas and nays taken thereon, and such proposed amendment or amendments must be disseminated to the public not more than 120 days before the next general election and not less than 90 days before the next general election as provided for by an</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> act</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Act</span><span> of the General Assembly; and if in the General Assembly next after the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> election such proposed amendment or amendments shall upon yea and nay vote be agreed to by two-thirds of all the members elected to each House, the same shall thereupon become part of the Constitution.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 183. Amend § 2, Article XVI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2. Constitutional Conventions; procedure; compensation of delegates; quorum; powers and duties; vacancies.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2. </span><span>The General Assembly by a two-thirds vote of all the members elected to each House may from time to time provide for the submission to the qualified electors of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State at the general election next thereafter the question, "Shall there be a Convention to revise the Constitution and amend the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">same"</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">same?”</span><span>; and upon such submission, if a majority of those voting on</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> question shall decide in favor of a Convention for such purpose, the General Assembly at its next session shall provide for the election of delegates to such Convention at the next general election. Such Convention shall be composed of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">forty-one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">41</span><span> delegates, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1 </span><span>of whom shall be chosen from each Representative District by the qualified electors thereof, and </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2 </span><span>of whom shall be chosen from New Castle County, </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2 </span><span>from Kent County and </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2 </span><span>from Sussex County by the qualified electors thereof respectively. The delegates so chosen shall convene at the Capital of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State on the first Tuesday in September next after their election. Every delegate shall receive for</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span> services such compensation as shall be provided by law. A majority of the Convention shall constitute a quorum for the transaction of business. The Convention shall have power to appoint such officers,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> employers</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> employees,</span><span> and assistants as it may deem necessary, and fix their compensation, and provide for the printing of its documents, journals,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> debates</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> debates,</span><span> and proceedings. The Convention shall determine the rules of its proceedings, and be the judge of the elections,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> returns</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> returns,</span><span> and qualification of its members. Whenever there shall be a vacancy in the office of delegate from any district or county by reason of failure to elect, ineligibility, death, resignation or otherwise, a writ of election to fill such vacancy shall be issued by the Governor, and such vacancy shall be filled by the qualified electors of such district or county. </span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 184. Amend § 3, Article XVI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 3. Receiving,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> tallying</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> tallying,</span><span> and counting votes for or against Convention; return of vote; enabling legislation.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 3. </span><span>The General Assembly shall provide for receiving,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> tallying</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> tallying,</span><span> and counting the votes for or against a Convention, and for returning to the General Assembly at its next session the state of such vote; and shall also enact all provisions necessary for giving effect to this Article.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 185. Amend § 4, Article XVI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 4. Approval of bills or resolutions under this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">article;</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Article; </span><span>exemption from </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Article III, section 18.</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section 18 of Article III.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 4. </span><span>No bill or resolution passed by the General Assembly under</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> or pursuant to</span><span> the provisions of this Article, shall require for its validity the approval of the Governor, and the same shall be exempt from the provisions of Section 18 of Article </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">III, of this Constitution.</span><span></span><span></span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">III.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 186. Amend § 5, Article XVI of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 5. Separate ballots on question of Convention.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 5. </span><span>In voting at any general election, upon the question, "Shall there be a Convention to revise the Constitution and amend the same?", the ballots shall be separate from those cast for any person voted for at such</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> election,</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> election</span><span> and shall be kept distinct and apart from all other ballots.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 187. Amend § 1, Article XVII of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. Continuity of state and local governmental operations in periods of emergency.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 1. </span><span>The General Assembly, in order to</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> insure</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> ensure</span><span> continuity of State and local governmental operations in periods of emergency resulting from enemy attack, terrorism, disease, accident, or other natural or man-made disaster, shall have the power and the immediate duty (1) to provide for prompt and temporary succession to the powers and duties of public offices whose immediate succession is not otherwise provided for by this Constitution, of whatever nature and whether filled by election or appointment, the incumbents of which may become unavailable for carrying on the powers and duties of such offices, and (2) to adopt such other measures as may be necessary and proper for </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">insuring</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">ensuring</span><span> the continuity of governmental operations. In the exercise of the powers conferred by this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section, </span><span>the General Assembly shall in all respects conform to the requirements of this Constitution except to the extent that in the judgment of the General Assembly to do so would be impracticable or would cause undue delay.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 188. Amend § 1, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 1. Delivery,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> filing</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> filing,</span><span> and publication of enrolled copy of amended Constitution and Schedule.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 1. </span><span>The President of this Convention, immediately on its adjournment, shall deliver the enrolled copy of this amended Constitution and Schedule to the Secretary of State, who shall file the same in</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the Secretary of State’s</span><span> office, and the Secretary of this Convention shall cause the same to be published </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">three</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">3</span><span> times in </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2</span><span> newspapers in each county of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 189. Amend § 2, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 2. Effective date of amended Constitution.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 2. </span><span>This amended Constitution shall take effect on the tenth day of June in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-seven.</span><span></span><span></span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1897.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 190. Amend § 3, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 3. Effect on offices of Senators and Representatives; election.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(a)</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 3. </span><span>The offices of the present Senators and Representatives shall not be vacated or otherwise affected by this amended Constitution, except that the Senators whose terms do not expire on the day of the next general election shall thereafter represent the districts in which they now reside until the end of the terms for which they were elected.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(b)</span><span> At the general election to be held in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-eight,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1898,</span><span> there shall be elected from each of the even numbered Senatorial Districts in</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State, except District number </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2</span><span> in New Castle County, District number </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">four</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">4</span><span> in Kent County, and District number </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2</span><span> in Sussex County, a Senator for the term of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2</span><span> years, and from each of the odd numbered Senatorial Districts in</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State a Senator for the term of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">four</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">4</span><span> years.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(c)</span><span> And thereafter, as the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> terms shall from time to time expire, a Senator shall be elected from each of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> Senatorial Districts for the full term of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">four</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">4</span><span> years.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(d)</span><span> At the general election to be held in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-eight,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1898,</span><span> there shall be elected in each Representative District in</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> the</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> this</span><span> State </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1</span><span> Representative for the term of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">two</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">2</span><span> years.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 191. Amend § 4, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 4. Commencement of terms of members of General Assembly.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>[Transferred to </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">§ 3</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section 3</span><span> of Article II].</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 192. Amend § 5, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 5. Date of first general election.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 5. </span><span>The first general election under this amended Constitution shall be held on the Tuesday next after the first Monday in the month of November in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-eight.</span><span></span><span></span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1898.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 193. Amend § 6, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 6. Effect on Governor's term.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 6. </span><span>The term of office of the present Governor shall not be vacated, or in any wise affected by this amended Constitution.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 194. Amend § 7, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 7. Continuation of elective and appointive offices; election of successors; renewal of official obligations.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 7. </span><span>Unless otherwise provided by this amended Constitution or Schedule, all persons elected or appointed before this amended Constitution shall take effect, to State or county offices made elective by this amended Constitution, whose terms will expire before the first Tuesday in January in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-nine,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1899, </span><span>shall hold their respective offices until the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> last mentioned day; and all persons elected or appointed as aforesaid to such offices, whose terms will expire between the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> first Tuesday in January in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-nine</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1899</span><span> and the first Tuesday in January in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand nine hundred and one,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1901, </span><span>shall hold their respective offices until the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> last mentioned day; and all persons elected or appointed as aforesaid to such offices, whose terms will expire between the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> first Tuesday in January in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand nine hundred and one</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1901</span><span> and the first Tuesday in January in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand nine hundred and three,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1903,</span><span> shall hold their respective offices until the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> last mentioned day; and the successors of such persons shall be elected at the general election next before the expiration of the terms as hereby extended; provided, however, that the successors of the present Auditor of Accounts, State</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Treasurer</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Treasurer,</span><span> and Insurance Commissioner shall be elected at the general election next preceding the expiration of their several terms of office, and the persons so elected shall enter upon the duties of their respective offices on the first Tuesday in January following their election. The officers whose terms of office are extended by this </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">section</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section</span><span> shall renew their official obligations upon the expiration of their present terms.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 195. Amend § 8, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 8. Date of commencement of terms of elective officers.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>[Transferred to </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">§ 24</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Section 24</span><span> of Article III.]</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 196. Amend § 9, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 9. Date of abolition of courts and judicial offices; transfer of pending proceedings and books,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> records</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> records,</span><span> and papers.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(a)</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 9.</span><span> All the courts of justice now existing shall continue with their present jurisdiction, and the Chancellor and judges shall continue in office until the tenth day of June in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-seven;</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1897; </span><span>upon which day the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> courts shall be abolished, and the offices of the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> Chancellor and judges shall expire.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(b)</span><span> All writs of error, and appeals and proceedings which, on the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> tenth day of June in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-seven</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1897</span><span> shall be depending in the Court of Errors and Appeals, and all the books,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> records</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> records,</span><span> and papers of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> court, shall be transferred to the Supreme Court established by this amended Constitution; and the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> writs of error,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> appeals</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> appeals,</span><span> and proceedings shall be proceeded in the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> Supreme Court to final judgment,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> decree</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> decree,</span><span> or other determination.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(c)</span><span> All suits, proceedings, and matters which, on the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> tenth day of June in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-seven,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1897, </span><span>shall be depending in the Superior Court, and all books,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> records</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> records,</span><span> and papers of the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said court,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">Court,</span><span> shall be transferred to the Superior Court established by this amended Constitution, and the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> suits,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> proceedings</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> proceedings,</span><span> and matters shall be proceeded in to final judgment, or determination, in the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> Superior Court established by this amended Constitution.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(d)</span><span> All indictments,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> proceedings</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> proceedings,</span><span> and matters which, on the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> tenth day of June in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-seven,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1897,</span><span> shall be depending in the Court of General Sessions of the Peace and Jail</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Delivery</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Delivery,</span><span> shall be transferred to and proceeded in to final judgment and determination in the Court of General Sessions established by this amended Constitution, or be otherwise disposed of by the Court of General Sessions, and all books,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> records</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> records,</span><span> and papers of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> Court of General Sessions of the Peace and Jail Delivery shall be transferred to the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> Court of General Sessions.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(e)</span><span> All indictments,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> proceedings</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> proceedings,</span><span> and matters which, on the </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> tenth day of June in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-seven,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1897,</span><span> shall be depending in the Court of Oyer and Terminer, shall be transferred to and proceeded in to final judgment and determination in the Court of Oyer and Terminer, established by this amended Constitution, and all books,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> records</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> records,</span><span> and papers of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> the</span><span> Court of Oyer and Terminer shall be transferred to </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">the </span><span>Court of Oyer and Terminer established by this amended Constitution.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: underline">(f)</span><span> All suits, proceedings and matters which, on the</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> said</span><span> tenth day of June in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-seven,</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1897, </span><span>shall be depending in the Court of Chancery, or in the Orphans' Court, and all records,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> books</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> books,</span><span> and papers of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">the</span><span> courts respectively, shall be transferred to Court of Chancery or Orphans' Court respectively, established by this amended Constitution; and the suits,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> proceedings</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> proceedings,</span><span> and matters, shall be proceeded in to final decree,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> order</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> order,</span><span> or other determination.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 197. Amend § 10, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 10. Registers' Court and jurisdiction of justice of the peace unaffected.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 10. </span><span>Unless otherwise provided, the Registers' Courts and the jurisdiction of Justice of the Peace shall not be affected by this amended Constitution.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 198. Amend § 11, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 11. Payments to certain incumbent judges not reappointed.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 11. </span><span>If the Chancellor,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Chief Justice</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Chief Justice,</span><span> or any Judge in office at the time this amended Constitution shall take effect shall not be appointed Chancellor,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> Chief Justice</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> Chief Justice,</span><span> or Judge under this amended Constitution,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> he or she</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> each</span><span> shall be entitled to receive the sum of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">fifteen hundred dollars</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">$1,500 </span><span>per annum, payable quarterly, for </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">five</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">5</span><span> years, after the expiration of</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> his or her office,</span><span></span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> if he or she shall so long live.</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> office, or until death, whichever occurs first.</span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 199. Amend § 12, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 12. First biennial session of General Assembly under Constitution.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 12. </span><span>The first biennial session of the General Assembly under this amended Constitution shall commence on the first Tuesday in January in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-nine.</span><span></span><span></span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1899.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 200. Amend § 13, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 13. Exceptions to limitations on amount of compensation payable to members of General Assembly and presiding officers.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 13. </span><span>The provisions of Section 15 of Article II of this amended Constitution limiting the amount of the compensation of the members of the General Assembly and the presiding officers of the respective Houses shall not apply to any adjourned,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> special</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> special,</span><span> or extra session of the General Assembly held </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">prior to</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">before</span><span> the first Tuesday in January in the year </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">one thousand eight hundred and ninety-nine.</span><span></span><span></span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">1899.</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 201. Amend § 14, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 14. Renewal of existing corporations until enactment of general incorporation law.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 14. </span><span>Until the General Assembly shall enact a general incorporation law as provided for in Section 1 of Article IX of this amended Constitution, existing corporations may, be renewed for a period not exceeding </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">four</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">4</span><span> years, without change or enlargement of their corporate powers or duties, in the manner lawful before this amended Constitution shall take effect.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 202. Amend § 15, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 15. Guardians' accounts.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 15. </span><span>Until the General Assembly shall otherwise provide, guardians' accounts shall be filed with and be adjusted and settled by the Register of Wills for the county, and be subject to exception, hearing,</span><span style="text-decoration: line-through"> adjustment</span><span style="text-decoration: underline"> adjustment,</span><span> and settlement in the Orphans' Court for the county as before this amended Constitution took effect.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 203. Amend § 16, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 16. Terms of office of persons holding office on effective date of Constitution.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 16. </span><span>Unless otherwise provided by this amended Constitution or Schedule, the terms of persons holding public offices to which they have been elected or appointed at the time this amended Constitution and Schedule shall take effect, shall not be vacated or otherwise affected thereby.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 204. Amend § 17, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 17. Vacancies in Board of Pardons.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 17. </span><span>One or more vacancies in the Board of Pardons shall not invalidate any act of the remaining members of </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">said</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">the </span><span>Board not less than </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">three</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">3</span><span> in number.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 205. Amend § 18, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 18. Laws consistent with Constitution not affected.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 18. </span><span>All the laws of this State existing at the time this Constitution shall take effect, and not inconsistent with </span><span style="text-decoration: line-through">it</span><span> </span><span style="text-decoration: underline">it,</span><span> shall remain in force, except so far as they shall be altered by future laws.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 206. Amend § 19, Schedule of the Delaware Constitution by making deletions as shown by strike through and insertions as shown by underline as follows:</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>§ 19. Enabling legislation.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span style="text-decoration: line-through">Section 19. </span><span>The General Assembly, as soon as conveniently may be after this Constitution shall take effect, shall enact all laws necessary or proper for carrying out the provisions thereof.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: 36pt; text-align: justify; line-height: 18pt"><span>Section 207. This Act adopts the headings for each Section of the Delaware Constitution as published by the Delaware Code Revisors and amended in this Act.</span><span></span><span></span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span> </span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-left: 21.6pt; margin-bottom: 0; text-indent: -21.6pt"><span>Approved March 25, 2025</span></p>
<p style="margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 0"><span> </span></p>
</div>
